# Skills · S · part 331

852 entries, structured-table-authoring to student-nest-ui. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **structured-table-authoring**: Use when a published figure or table encodes per-system feature assessments (e.g., check/cross/partial notation with footnoted qualifiers) and the goal is to reproduce it as a parseable CSV with one row per system and explicit qualifier text. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/structured-table-authoring))
- **structured-table-construction**: Use when a survey paper proposes a canonical multi-stage framework or taxonomy and the analysis goal is to distill it into a machine-readable keyed table (e.g., CSV) where each row pairs a stage/entity with its assigned methodology or class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-agentic-ai/tree/HEAD/collections/agentic-ai/v1/leaves/structured-table-construction))
- **structured-table-construction**: Use when a repository manifest or setup script must be enumerated into a structured table of tool names plus pipeline-stage categories, and the resulting count must be compared against counts stated in the paper's abstract or revision history. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/structured-table-construction))
- **structured-table-construction-from-reported-figures**: Use when a paper reports counts and approximate percentages only in prose (e.g. '118 out of 233 eukaryotic OTUs', '~55% Fungi') and they must be consolidated into a structured CSV profile table for reuse. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/structured-table-construction-from-reported-figures))
- **structured-table-curation**: Use when a published article tabulates per-entity configuration values (e.g., per-technology cell barcode length, UMI length, and special-case flags) that must be reproduced as a structured, row-per-item table for downstream automation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/structured-table-curation))
- **structured-table-data-extraction**: Use when an article presents a qualitative feature-comparison matrix (e.g., tool-vs-requirement support levels in Table 1) that must be turned into a validated, structured file like table1_comparison.tsv with consistent encodings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/structured-table-data-extraction))
- **structured-table-serialization-csv-json**: Use when a task asks to reproduce a published parameter table — e.g. bar pattern speed 37 km/s/kpc, two-armed spiral 13.1 km/s/kpc (~25% contrast), three-armed 16.4 km/s/kpc (~9% contrast) — as a machine-readable CSV/JSON artifact. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/structured-table-serialization-csv-json))

Use when [structured task list] is needed. ([bianhaifeng789-hue/openclaw-config](https://github.com/bianhaifeng789-hue/openclaw-config/tree/HEAD/skills-experimental/structured-task-list))
- **structured-tasks**: Routes Promptify task construction, prompt building, parsing, async and batch execution, custom Pydantic schemas, and provider-side task failures. - **structured-tech-report**: 工程／研究修改的結構化技術報告 skill — 把「問題→根因→修改→驗證→影響」流程整理為 13 段式報告，給「具備基本背景但不熟此專案」的讀者讀。整合 Toyota A3 + ADR + Google SRE Postmortem + Diátaxis 多受眾分層；繼承 InterSubMod doc-standards／v5_audit_suite 範本。USE WHEN：「寫技術報告」「整理修改」「13段報告」「self-phasing 報告」「pipeline 變更說明」「PR/commit 整理」「整理 bug fix」「整理工程改動」。與 results-report（實驗結果分析）／weekly-report（週進度多主題）／conclude-research（研究收尾假說驗證）／report（AI 對話紀錄）責任不同：本 skill 專責**單一工程／流程修改的深度敘事**。因果鏈技術報告必繼承 InterSubMod/.claude/skills/scientific-rigor/SKILL.md §2-§7 + §9.2（§6 因果鏈每環標 L1-L5 + mermaid DAG 標 confounder、NEGATIVE 結論走 SRE blameless 5-段格式）。SKIP WHEN 多主題週進度（用 weekly-report）、實驗結果報告（用… ([liaoyoyo/InterSubMod](https://github.com/liaoyoyo/InterSubMod/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/structured-tech-report) · ★1)

- **structured-thinking**: 将复杂问题拆解为层次分明、不重叠不遗漏的结构框架。适用于需要梳理混乱信息、向他人清晰表达、全面分析问题的场景。当你面对一堆散乱的要素无法形成全局视图，或需要向他人做结构化汇报时使用此skill。不适用于已经高度结构化的问题或需要直觉判断的快速决策场景。关键触发信号："信息太多理不清"、"怎么说才清楚"、"有没有遗漏"、"帮我拆解这个问题"。 ([kangarooking/cognitive-dividend-skill](https://github.com/kangarooking/cognitive-dividend-skill/tree/HEAD/structured-thinking) · ★59)
- **structured-thinking**: 结构化思维专家助手。在解决复杂问题、设计方案、分析决策时，提供系统化的思维框架，减少AI幻觉和逻辑错误，提高输出质量和可靠性。 ([dkbnull/hello-skill](https://github.com/dkbnull/hello-skill/tree/HEAD/common/skills/structured-thinking) · ★44)
- **structured-thinking**: Verwenden, wenn eine unübersichtliche oder folgenreiche Lage die ganze Kette in einem Durchgang braucht: das eigentliche Problem verstehen, Optionen erzeugen, dann entscheiden: 'hilf mir, das durchzudenken', 'von der Ursache bis zur Entscheidung', 'strukturiertes Denken', 'Problem, Ideen, Entscheidung', 'ich hänge fest, ob ich ... soll', 'keiner weiß, warum ..., und ich muss dem Vorstand etwas sagen', 'erst analysieren, dann Optionen, dann empfehlen', /structured-thinking. Meta-Ablauf in drei Phasen: analysieren (think), Ideen entwickeln (brainstorm), entscheiden (decide); lässt eine nicht nö… ([ellmos-ai/skills](https://github.com/ellmos-ai/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/utilities/structured-thinking) · ★9)
- **structured-thinking**: 结构化思考 / Structured thinking。用于问题澄清、概念边界、认知路由、信息缺口、下一步判断和先想清楚再行动。 ([ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition](https://github.com/ArchSightLabs/archsight-cognition/tree/HEAD/skills/structured-thinking) · ★8)
- **structured-thinking**: 使用苏格拉底提问、第一性原理和奥卡姆剃刀三种思维框架，系统性分析产品想法、需求、技术方案或日常决策。当用户说"分析这个需求"、"分析这个产品"、"帮我用结构化思考分析"、"分析一下这个决策"、或直接描述一个产品/需求/决策想法时触发。适用于软件开发、产品设计、业务决策等任何需要深入分析的领域。 ([Herxinsasa/Flux](https://github.com/Herxinsasa/Flux/tree/HEAD/skills-user/packages/structured-thinking) · ★6 · 3 copies)

([jadmadi/skills](https://github.com/jadmadi/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/structured-thinking) · ★1)

- **structured-thinking**: 構造を整理し観点を切り分け命名と境界を精緻化するスキル。構造を提案する場面、命名を考える場面、複数案を比較する場面、判断軸を立てる場面、問題を分解する場面、議論の論点が複数混ざってきた場面では、ユーザーが明示的に依頼していなくても必ず使うこと。具体的にはアーキテクチャ提案、ディレクトリ構成検討、コンポーネント分割、エージェント・スキルの境界決定、変数・関数・ファイル名選定、評価軸列挙、要件分解、論点整理など、「単位を切り出す」または「軸を立てる」行為を行う直前で発動する。曖昧なまま進めると後段で必ず手戻りが発生するので、テンポを落としてでも先に通すこと。 ([mtkhs/dotfiles](https://github.com/mtkhs/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/structured-thinking) · ★1)

- **structured-vs-unstructured-routing**: Decides what shape a source actually has before any analysis is chosen, separating a clean table from records whose fields differ from one another and from free text that has no columns at all. (한국어 전용) ([EndoRobotics-Co-LTD/endo-skills](https://github.com/EndoRobotics-Co-LTD/endo-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/structured-writing))
